covering 4637 curated list occurrences. Repeated URLs share an entity,
retain all source occurrences in frontmatter, and appear in every source category.

Run `python3 tools/ingest.py --date 2026-11-04` to reproduce the import from
the immutable captured README. Use `--source` for a newer upstream checkout.

The list is canon. Resource status remains unknown until checked; source descriptions
are attributed claims, not independently verified assessments. Linked projects and
lists are not recursively imported.

### Aggregators

- [inth3shadows/terse](wiki/inth3shadows-terse.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/inth3shadows/terse>) — Transparent proxy that sits in front of any other MCP server and shrinks its tool output before it reaches the model. Lossless by default — a denser encoding of the same bytes, with a round-trip gate asserting an exact inverse, so nothing is dropped or summarised. Repeated calls to the same tool emit a delta against the prior result instead of the whole payload. Per-tool opt-in, no decode step, no model in the loop. `uv tool install terse-mcp` then `terse proxy -- `
- [402signalhq/402signal](wiki/402signalhq-402signal.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/402signalhq/402signal>) — Checks and validates live x402 payment routes across Base, Solana and Algorand.
- [GarphenGate/moltline-mcp](wiki/garphengate-moltline-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/GarphenGate/moltline-mcp>) — Bridge to Moltline Studio's hosted MCP servers: code review, agent governance, business math, regulatory deadlines, crypto tax lots, routing and optimization.
- [team886/findagent-mcp](wiki/team886-findagent-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/team886/findagent-mcp>) — Marketplace of vetted agents with automated security scans plus human review, credential-to-host binding and sandboxed hosted code with default-deny egress.
- [BotHireAgent/BotHireMCPServer](wiki/bothireagent-bothiremcpserver.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/BotHireAgent/BotHireMCPServer>) — BotHire agent-to-agent hiring marketplace: search agents and skills by capability and trust score, read live demand, and settle in USDT or USDC across chains.
- [neoninnovationlab/neon-mcp-gateway](wiki/neoninnovationlab-neon-mcp-gateway.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/neoninnovationlab/neon-mcp-gateway>) — Edge firewall for exposing internal PostgreSQL databases and APIs to AI agents with strict validation.
- [daedalusdevelopmentgroup/ddg-agent-payable-services](wiki/daedalusdevelopmentgroup-ddg-agent-payable-services.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/daedalusdevelopmentgroup/ddg-agent-payable-services>) — Pay-per-call x402 tools (utilities, DNS/WHOIS, blockchain RPC, market and DEX data, prediction markets, security audits) plus an OpenAI-compatible LLM gateway.
- [forgemeshlabs/coinopai-mcp](wiki/forgemeshlabs-coinopai-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/forgemeshlabs/coinopai-mcp>) — Paid crypto intelligence via x402 on Base: preflight checks, trade decisions audited against real prices, risk state, signal history and automation search.
- [forgemeshlabs/anomaly-mcp](wiki/forgemeshlabs-anomaly-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/forgemeshlabs/anomaly-mcp>) — Real-time anomaly detection with NASA-derived sequence mining across blockchain, mempool, stablecoin depeg, aviation and GitHub signals, paid via x402 on Base.

- [2s-io/sdk](wiki/2s-io-sdk.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/2s-io/sdk>) — Pay-per-call x402 API: geocoding, weather, earthquakes, patents, US case law, Federal Register, scientific papers, DNS/WHOIS, OFAC screening and Census data.

- [BerkantACUN/ifc-ruhsat](wiki/berkantacun-ifc-ruhsat.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/BerkantACUN/ifc-ruhsat>) — Checks an IFC building model against Türkiye's building-permit BIM regulation (Official Gazette 33331, in force Sept 2027): mandatory IFC 4.3 classes and category codes, per-class attribute and property-set requirements (all 68 annex tables, incl. the Türkiye-specific `TREpys_` sets), TUREF coordinate system, naming templates and space geometry — and fills in the regulation's 21-row Annex-9 quality-control form. Findings cite the article and annex table with GlobalIds; rows a machine cannot verify are reported as manual, never as passed. Also exports buildingSMART IDS. `uvx ifc-ruhsat mcp`; on the official registry as `io.github.BerkantACUN/ifc-ruhsat`.

- [DevHusnainAi/zerodom](wiki/devhusnainai-zerodom.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/DevHusnainAi/zerodom>) — Deterministic attack-surface perception for AI security agents. Attaches over `chrome.debugger` to the Chrome you're already logged into (cookies, MFA and SSO already done) and turns the live page into a short numbered interaction graph an agent can act on (`\[03\] button 'Sign In'`), with CSS selectors kept server-side and out of the model's context. Cross-identity tools (`compare_identities`, `replay`) flag a byte-identical response across two logged-in users as a cross-tenant IDOR, and `scan` maps the attack surface from the CLI. No LLM in the parse; 71% fewer tokens than Playwright's ARIA snapshot mode.

### Coding Agents

- [ly8427/mcp-review-board](wiki/ly8427-mcp-review-board.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ly8427/mcp-review-board>) — Localhost review board where different coding agents (Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, Trae, ZCode) review each other's work: objections must state flip conditions, revisions clear all verdicts, resolution requires the whole active quorum, and every vote is append-only audited. - [dockndevai/mcp-outlook](wiki/dockndevai-mcp-outlook.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/dockndevai/mcp-outlook>) — Outlook mail via Microsoft Graph: read, search, draft, send and organize email. Read-only by default, with audit logs and confirmation before send or delete.
